\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2})\|P-\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|_{F}^{2}}_{C_{4}}\penalty\ +\underbrace{(\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}-\alpha_{2}^{2})\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}}_{C_{5}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ (\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2})\|P-\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|_{F}^{2}\penalty\ +(\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}-\alpha_{2}^{2})\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \min\{(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}),(\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}-\alpha_{2}^{2})\}\cdot(\|P-\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|_{F}^{2}+\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \min\{(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}),(\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}-\alpha_{2}^{2})\}\cdot(\|P-\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|_{F}^{2}+\|\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|^{2})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \min\{(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}),(\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}-\alpha_{2}^{2})\}\cdot\|P\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \rho\|P\|_{F}^{2},

where the first step follows by
B=B_{1}+B_{2}+B_{3}
, and the second step follows by the definition of
A,B_{1},B_{2},B_{3}
the third step follows by
A+B_{1}+B_{2}+B_{3}=C_{1}+C_{2}+C_{3}+C_{4}+C_{5}
, the fourth step follows by
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}\geq 0
, the fifth step follows
a\geq\min(a,b)
, the sixth step follows by
\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}=\|\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|^{2}
, the seventh step follows by triangle inequality, and the last step follows the definition of
\rho
.
∎

Claim D.5
.

A+B_{1}+B_{2}+B_{3}=C_{1}+C_{2}+C_{3}+C_{4}+C_{5}
.

Proof.

The key properties we need are, for two vectors
a,b
,
\|a+b\|^{2}=\|a\|^{2}+2\langle a,b\rangle+\|b\|^{2}
; for two matrices
A,B
,
\|A+B\|_{F}^{2}=\|A\|_{F}^{2}+2\langle A,B\rangle+\|B\|_{F}^{2}
.
Then, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\ C_{1}+C_{2}+C_{3}+C_{4}+C_{5}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{(\|\alpha_{0}P\cdot{\mathbf{1}}\|)^{2}+2(\alpha_{0}\alpha_{2}-\alpha_{0}^{2})\langle P\cdot{\mathbf{1}},\mathrm{diag}(P)\rangle+(\alpha_{2}-\alpha_{0})^{2}\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}}_{C_{1}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{1}^{2}(\mathrm{diag}(P)^{\top}\cdot{\mathbf{1}})^{2}}_{C_{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2}(2\|P\|_{F}^{2}+4\|\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|_{F}^{2}+2\langle P,P^{\top}\rangle-4\langle P,\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\rangle-4\langle P^{\top},\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\rangle)}_{C_{3}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2})(\|P\|_{F}^{2}-2\langle P,\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\rangle+\|\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|_{F}^{2})}_{C_{4}}+\underbrace{(\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}-\alpha_{2}^{2})\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}}_{C_{5}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{\alpha_{0}^{2}\|P\cdot{\mathbf{1}}\|^{2}+2(\alpha_{0}\alpha_{2}-\alpha_{0}^{2})\langle P\cdot{\mathbf{1}},\mathrm{diag}(P)\rangle+(\alpha_{2}-\alpha_{0})^{2}\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}}_{C_{1}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{1}^{2}(\mathrm{diag}(P)^{\top}\cdot{\mathbf{1}})^{2}}_{C_{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2}(2\|P\|_{F}^{2}+4\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}+2\langle P,P^{\top}\rangle-8\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2})}_{C_{3}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2})(\|P\|_{F}^{2}-2\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}+\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2})}_{C_{4}}+\underbrace{(\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}-\alpha_{2}^{2})\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}}_{C_{5}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \alpha_{0}^{2}\|P\cdot{\mathbf{1}}\|^{2}+2(\alpha_{0}\alpha_{2}-\alpha_{0}^{2})\mathrm{diag}(P)^{\top}\cdot P\cdot{\mathbf{1}}+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\mathrm{diag}(P)^{\top}\cdot{\mathbf{1}})^{2}+\alpha_{1}^{2}\langle P,P^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ (\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2})\|P\|_{F}^{2}+\underbrace{((\alpha_{2}-\alpha_{0})^{2}-2\alpha_{1}^{2}-\beta_{0}+\alpha_{0}^{2}+\alpha_{1}^{2}+\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}^{2}-\alpha_{2}^{2})}_{\beta_{2}-\beta_{0}-2(\alpha_{2}\alpha_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}+\alpha_{1}^{2})}\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{0}_{\text{part\penalty\ of\penalty\ }A}+\underbrace{2(\alpha_{2}\alpha_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2})\cdot\mathrm{diag}(P)^{\top}P\cdot{\mathbf{1}}}_{\text{part\penalty\ of\penalty\ }B_{1}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{1}^{2}\cdot((\mathrm{diag}(P)^{\top}{\mathbf{1}})^{2}+\langle P,P^{\top}\rangle)}_{\text{part\penalty\ of\penalty\ }B_{2}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{0}^{2}\cdot\|P\cdot{\mathbf{1}}\|^{2}}_{\text{part\penalty\ of\penalty\ }B_{3}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2})\cdot\|P\|_{F}^{2}}_{\text{proportional\penalty\ to\penalty\ }\|P\|_{F}^{2}}+\underbrace{(\beta_{2}-\beta_{0}-2(\alpha_{2}\alpha_{0}-\alpha_{0}^{2}+\alpha_{1}^{2}))\cdot\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}}_{\text{proportional\penalty\ to\penalty\ }\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{(\beta_{2}-\beta_{0})\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}+\beta_{0}\|P\|_{F}^{2}}_{A}+\underbrace{2(\alpha_{0}\alpha_{2}-\alpha_{0}^{2})(\mathrm{diag}(P)^{\top}\cdot P\cdot{\bf 1}-\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2})}_{B_{1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underbrace{\alpha_{1}^{2}((\mathrm{diag}(P)^{\top}\cdot{\bf 1})^{2}-\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}+\langle P,P^{\top}\rangle-\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2})}_{B_{2}}+\underbrace{\alpha_{0}^{2}(\|P\cdot{\bf 1}\|^{2}-\|P\|_{F}^{2})}_{B_{3}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A+B_{1}+B_{2}+B_{3}

where the second step follows by
\langle P,\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\rangle=\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}
and
\|\mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{diag}(P))\|_{F}^{2}=\|\mathrm{diag}(P)\|^{2}
.
∎

D.2.2
Lower Bound on the Eigenvalues of Hessian for Non-orthogonal Case

First we show the lower bound of the eigenvalues. The main idea is to reduce the problem to a
k
-by-
k
problem and then lower bound the eigenvalues using orthogonal weight matrices.

Lemma D.6

(Lower bound)
.

If
\phi(z)
satisfies Property
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
, we have the following property for the second derivative of function
f_{\cal D}(W)
at
W^{*}
,

\displaystyle\Omega(v_{\min}^{2}\rho(\sigma_{k})/(\kappa^{2}\lambda))I\preceq\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W^{*}).

Proof.

Let
a\in\mathbb{R}^{dk}
denote vector
\begin{bmatrix}a_{1}^{\top}&a_{2}^{\top}&\cdots&a_{k}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}
, let
b\in\mathbb{R}^{dk}
denote vector
\begin{bmatrix}b_{1}^{\top}&b_{2}^{\top}&\cdots&b_{k}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}
and let
c\in\mathbb{R}^{dk}
denote vector
\begin{bmatrix}c_{1}^{\top}&c_{2}^{\top}&\cdots&c_{k}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}
.
The smallest eigenvalue of the Hessian can be calculated by

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f(W^{*})

\displaystyle\succeq\min_{\|a\|=1}a^{\top}\nabla^{2}f(W^{*})a\penalty\ I_{dk}=\min_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}v_{i}^{*}a_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]\penalty\ I_{dk}

(9)

Note that

\displaystyle\penalty\ \min_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}(v_{i}^{*}a_{i})^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \min_{\|a\|\neq 0}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}(v_{i}^{*}a_{i})^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]/\|a\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \min_{\sum_{i}\|b_{i}/v_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\neq 0}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]/\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|b_{i}/v_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\text{\penalty\ by\penalty\ }a_{i}=b_{i}/v_{i}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \min_{\sum_{i}\|b_{i}\|^{2}\neq 0}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]/\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|b_{i}/v_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\min}^{2}\min_{\sum_{i}\|b_{i}\|^{2}\neq 0}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]/\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|b_{i}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\text{\penalty\ by\penalty\ }v_{\min}=\min_{i\in[k]}|v_{i}^{*}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\min}^{2}\min_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}a_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]

(10)

Let
U\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
be the orthonormal basis of
W^{*}
and let
V=\begin{bmatrix}v_{1}&v_{2}&\cdots&v_{k}\end{bmatrix}=U^{\top}W^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times k}
.
Also note that
V
and
W^{*}
have same singular values and
W^{*}=UV
. We use
U_{\bot}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times(d-k)}
to denote the complement of
U
. For any vector
a_{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, there exist two vectors
b_{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}
and
c_{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{d-k}
such that

\displaystyle a_{j}=Ub_{j}+U_{\bot}c_{j}.

We use
{\cal D}_{d}
to denote Gaussian distribution
{\cal N}(0,I_{d})
,
{\cal D}_{d-k}
to denote Gaussian distribution
{\cal N}(0,I_{d-k})
, and
{\cal D}_{k}
to denote Gaussian distribution
{\cal N}(0,I_{k})
. Then we can rewrite formulation (
10
) (removing
v_{\min}^{2}
) as

\displaystyle\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}a_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]=\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}(b_{i}^{\top}U^{\top}+c_{i}^{\top}U_{\perp}^{\top})x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]=A+B+C

where

\displaystyle A=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}U^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle B=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}c_{i}^{\top}U_{\perp}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle C=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[2\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}U^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)\cdot\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}c_{i}^{\top}U_{\perp}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)\right].

We calculate
A,B,C
separately. First, we can show

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}U^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]=\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}z\cdot\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)\right)^{2}\right].

Second, we can show

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}c_{i}^{\top}U_{\perp}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\underset{s\sim{\cal D}_{d-k},z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}c_{i}^{\top}s\cdot\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\underset{s\sim{\cal D}_{d-k},z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}[(y^{\top}s)^{2}]

\displaystyle\text{\penalty\ by\penalty\ defining\penalty\ }y=\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)c_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d-k}

\displaystyle=\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\underset{s\sim{\cal D}_{d-k}}{\mathbb{E}}[(y^{\top}s)^{2}]\right]

\displaystyle=\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\underset{s\sim{\cal D}_{d-k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{j=1}^{d-k}s_{j}^{2}y_{j}^{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\text{\penalty\ by\penalty\ }\mathbb{E}[s_{j}s_{j^{\prime}}]=0

\displaystyle=\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{j=1}^{d-k}y_{j}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\text{\penalty\ by\penalty\ }s_{j}\sim{\cal N}(0,1)

\displaystyle=\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)c_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\text{\penalty\ by\penalty\ definition\penalty\ of\penalty\ }y

Third, we have
C=0
since
U_{\perp}^{\top}x
is independent of
w_{i}^{*\top}x
and
U^{\top}x
. Thus, putting them all together,

\displaystyle\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}a_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]=\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}z\cdot\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)\right)^{2}\right]+\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)c_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]

Let us lower bound
A
,

\displaystyle A=

\displaystyle\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}z\cdot\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}z\cdot\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)\right)^{2}e^{-\|z\|^{2}/2}dz

\displaystyle\underset{\xi_{1}}{=}

\displaystyle\int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}V^{\dagger\top}s\cdot\phi^{\prime}(s_{i})\right)^{2}e^{-\|V^{\dagger\top}s\|^{2}/2}\cdot|\det(V^{\dagger})|ds

\displaystyle\underset{\xi_{2}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}V^{\dagger\top}s\cdot\phi^{\prime}(s_{i})\right)^{2}e^{-\sigma^{2}_{1}(V^{\dagger})\|s\|^{2}/2}\cdot|\det(V^{\dagger})|ds

\displaystyle\underset{\xi_{3}}{=}

\displaystyle\int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}V^{\dagger\top}u/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})\cdot\phi^{\prime}(u_{i}/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger}))\right)^{2}e^{-\|u\|^{2}/2}|\det(V^{\dagger})|/\sigma_{1}^{k}(V^{\dagger})du

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}p_{i}^{\top}u\cdot\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})\right)^{2}e^{-\|u\|^{2}/2}\frac{1}{\lambda}du

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\underset{u\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}p_{i}^{\top}u\cdot\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})\right)^{2}\right]

where
V^{\dagger}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times k}
is the inverse of
V\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times k}
, i.e.,
V^{\dagger}V=I
,
p_{i}^{\top}=b_{i}^{\top}V^{\dagger\top}/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})
and
\sigma_{k}=\sigma_{k}(W^{*})
.
\xi_{1}
replaces
z
by
z=V^{\dagger\top}s
, so
v_{i}^{*\top}z=s_{i}
.
\xi_{2}
uses the fact
\|V^{\dagger\top}s\|\leq\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})\|s\|
.
\xi_{3}
replaces
s
by
s=u/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})
. Note that
\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})
’s are independent of each other, so we can simplify the analysis.
In particular, Lemma
D.4
gives a lower bound in this case in terms of
p_{i}
.
Note that
\|p_{i}\|\geq\|b_{i}\|/\kappa
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}b_{i}^{\top}z\cdot\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)\right)^{2}\right]\geq\rho(\sigma_{k})\frac{1}{\kappa^{2}\lambda}\|b\|^{2}.

For
B
, similar to the proof of Lemma
D.4
, we have,

\displaystyle B=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)c_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)c_{i}\right\|^{2}e^{-\|z\|^{2}/2}dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})c_{i}\right\|^{2}e^{-\|V^{\dagger\top}u/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})\|^{2}/2}\cdot\det(V^{\dagger}/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger}))du

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})c_{i}\right\|^{2}e^{-\|V^{\dagger\top}u/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})\|^{2}/2}\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda}du

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(2\pi)^{-k/2}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})c_{i}\right\|^{2}e^{-\|u\|^{2}/2}\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda}du

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{1}{\lambda}\underset{u\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})c_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\underset{u\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})c_{i}^{\top}c_{i}]+\sum_{i\neq l}\underset{u\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{l})c_{i}^{\top}c_{l}]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot u_{i})^{2}]\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|c_{i}\|^{2}+\left(\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot z)]\right)^{2}\sum_{i\neq l}c_{i}^{\top}c_{l}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\left(\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot z)]\right)^{2}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}c_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left(\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot z)^{2}]-\left(\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot z)]\right)^{2}\right)\|c\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot z)^{2}]-\left(\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{k}\cdot z)]\right)^{2}\right)\|c\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \rho(\sigma_{k})\frac{1}{\lambda}\|c\|^{2},

where the first step follows by definition of Gaussian distribution, the second step follows by replacing
z
by
z=V^{\dagger\top}u/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})
, and then
v_{i}^{\top}z=u_{i}/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})=u_{i}\sigma_{k}(W^{*})
, the third step follows by
\|u\|^{2}\geq\|\frac{1}{\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})}{V^{\dagger}}^{\top}u\|^{2}
, the fourth step follows by
\det(V^{\dagger}/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger}))=\det(V^{\dagger})/\sigma_{1}^{k}(V^{\dagger})=1/\lambda
, the fifth step follows by definition of Gaussian distribution, the ninth step follows by
x^{2}\geq 0
for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
, and the last step follows by Property
3.2
.

Note that
1=\|a\|^{2}=\|b\|^{2}+\|c\|^{2}
. Thus, we finish the proof for the lower bound.
∎

D.2.3
Upper Bound on the Eigenvalues of Hessian for Non-orthogonal Case

Lemma D.7

(Upper bound)
.

If
\phi(z)
satisfies Property
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
, we have the following property for the second derivative of function
f_{\cal D}(W)
at
W^{*}
,

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W^{*})\preceq O(kv_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p})I.

Proof.

Similarly, we can calculate the upper bound of the eigenvalues by

\displaystyle\penalty\ \|\nabla^{2}f(W^{*})\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}a^{\top}\nabla^{2}f(W^{*})a

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}a_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sum_{l=1}^{k}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}[a_{i}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\cdot a_{l}^{\top}x\cdot\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sum_{l=1}^{k}\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}[(a_{i}^{\top}x)^{4}]\cdot\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x))^{4}]\cdot\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}[(a_{l}^{\top}x)^{4}]\cdot\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x))^{4}]\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sum_{l=1}^{k}\|a_{i}\|\cdot\|a_{l}\|\cdot\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p}\cdot\|w_{l}^{*}\|^{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sum_{l=1}^{k}\|a_{i}\|\cdot\|a_{l}\|\cdot\sigma_{1}^{2p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ kv_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p},

where the first inequality follows Hölder’s inequality, the second inequality follows by Property
3.1
, the third inequality follows by
\|w_{i}^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{1}(W^{*})
, and the last inequality by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

∎

D.3
Error Bound of Hessians near the Ground Truth for Smooth Activations

The goal of this Section is to prove Lemma
D.8

Lemma D.8

(Error bound of Hessians near the ground truth for smooth activations)
.

Let
\phi(z)
satisfy Property
3.1
, Property
3.2
and Property
3.3
(a). Let
W
satisfy
\|W-W^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{k}/2
. Let
S
denote a set of i.i.d. samples from the distribution defined in (
1
). Then for any
t\geq 1
and
0<\epsilon<1/2
, if

\displaystyle|S|\geq\epsilon^{-2}d\kappa^{2}\tau\cdot\poly(\log d,t)

then we have, with probability at least
1-1/d^{\Omega(t)}
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)-\nabla^{2}f_{\D}(W^{*})\|\lesssim v_{\max}^{2}k\sigma_{1}^{p}(\epsilon\sigma_{1}^{p}+\|W-W^{*}\|).

Proof.

This follows by combining Lemma
D.10
and Lemma
D.11
directly.
∎

D.3.1
Second-order Smoothness near the Ground Truth for Smooth Activations

The goal of this Section is to prove Lemma
D.10
.

Fact D.9
.

Let
w_{i}
denote the
i
-th column of
W\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
, and
w_{i}^{*}
denote the
i
-th column of
W^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
. If
\|W-W^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{k}(W^{*})/2
, then for all
i\in[k]
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|w_{i}^{*}\|\leq\|w_{i}\|\leq\frac{3}{2}\|w_{i}^{*}\|.

Proof.

Note that if
\|W-W^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{k}(W^{*})/2
, we have
\sigma_{k}(W^{*})/2\leq\sigma_{i}(W)\leq\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{1}(W^{*})
for all
i\in[k]
by Weyl’s inequality. By definition of singular value, we have
\sigma_{k}(W^{*})\leq\|w_{i}^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{1}(W^{*})
. By definition of spectral norm, we have
\|w_{i}-w_{i}^{*}\|\leq\|W-W^{*}\|
.
Thus, we can lower bound
\|w_{i}\|
,

\displaystyle\|w_{i}\|\leq\|w_{i}^{*}\|+\|w_{i}-w_{i}^{*}\|\leq\|w_{i}^{*}\|+\|W-W^{*}\|\leq\|w_{i}^{*}\|+\sigma_{k}/2\leq\frac{3}{2}\|w_{i}^{*}\|.

Similarly, we have
\|w_{i}\|\geq\frac{1}{2}\|w_{i}^{*}\|
.
∎

Lemma D.10

(Second-order smoothness near the ground truth for smooth activations)
.

If
\phi(z)
satisfies Property
3.1
, Property
3.2
and Property
3.3
(a),
then for any
W
with
\|W-W^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{k}/2
, we have

\|\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W)-\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W^{*})\|\lesssim k^{2}v_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{p}\|W-W^{*}\|.

Proof.

Let
\Delta=\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W)-\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W^{*})
. For each
(i,l)\in[k]\times[k]
, let
\Delta_{i,l}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
denote the
(i,l)
-th block of
\Delta
.
Then, for
i\neq l
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,l}=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\left(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)\right)xx^{\top}\right],

and for
i=l
, we have

\displaystyle\penalty\ \Delta_{i,i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{r=1}^{k}v_{r}^{*}\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}+v^{*2}_{i}\left(\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)xx^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{r=1}^{k}v_{r}^{*}\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right]+\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[v^{*2}_{i}\left(\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\right)xx^{\top}\right].

(11)

In the next a few paragraphs, we first show how to bound the off-diagonal term, and then show how to bound the diagonal term.

First, we consider off-diagonal terms.

\displaystyle\penalty\ \|\Delta_{i,l}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left\|\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\left(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)\right)xx^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)\right|\cdot(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|+|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)|\right)(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\penalty\ +\left.\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[L_{2}|(w_{i}-w_{i}^{*})^{\top}x||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]+\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)|L_{2}|(w_{l}-w_{l}^{*})^{\top}x|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[L_{2}|(w_{i}-w_{i}^{*})^{\top}x|L_{1}|w_{l}^{\top}x|^{p}(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]+\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[L_{1}|w_{i}^{*\top}x|^{p}L_{2}|(w_{l}-w_{l}^{*})^{\top}x|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}L_{1}L_{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[|(w_{i}-w_{i}^{*})^{\top}x||w_{l}^{\top}x|^{p}(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]+\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[|(w_{l}-w_{l}^{*})^{\top}x||w_{i}^{*\top}x|^{p}(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}L_{1}L_{2}\max_{\|a\|=1}(\|w_{i}-w_{i}^{*}\|\|w_{l}\|^{p}\|a\|^{2}+\|w_{l}-w_{l}^{*}\|\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p}\|a\|^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}L_{1}L_{2}\sigma_{1}^{p}(W^{*})\|W-W^{*}\|

(12)

where the first step follows by definition of
\Delta_{i,l}
, the second step follows by definition of spectral norm and
v_{i}^{*}v_{l}^{*}\leq v_{\max}^{2}
, the third step follows by triangle inequality, the fourth step follows by linearity of expectation, the fifth step follows by Property
3.3
(a), i.e.,
|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)|\leq L_{2}|(w_{i}-w_{i}^{*})^{\top}x|
, the sixth step follows by Property
3.1
, i.e.,
\phi^{\prime}(z)\leq L_{1}|z|^{p}
, the seventh step follows by Fact
B.6
, and the last step follows by
\|a\|^{2}=1
,
\|w_{i}-w_{i}^{*}\|\leq\|W-W^{*}\|
,
\|w_{i}\|\leq\frac{3}{2}\|w_{i}^{*}\|
, and
\|w_{i}^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{1}(W^{*})
.

Note that the proof for the off-diagonal terms also applies to bounding the second-term in the diagonal block Eq. (
D.3.1
).
Thus we only need to show how to bound the first term in the diagonal block Eq. (
D.3.1
).

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left\|\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{r=1}^{k}v_{r}^{*}\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left\|\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\sum_{r=1}^{k}v_{r}^{*}(\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-\phi(w_{r}^{*\top}x))\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\sum_{r=1}^{k}\max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[|\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-\phi(w_{r}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}\sum_{r=1}^{k}\max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[|\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-\phi(w_{r}^{*\top}x)|L_{2}(x^{\top}a)^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}L_{2}\sum_{r=1}^{k}\max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\max_{z\in[w_{r}^{\top}x,w_{r}^{*\top}x]}|\phi^{\prime}(z)|\cdot|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\cdot(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}L_{2}\sum_{r=1}^{k}\max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\max_{z\in[w_{r}^{\top}x,w_{r}^{*\top}x]}L_{1}|z|^{p}\cdot|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\cdot(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}L_{1}L_{2}\sum_{r=1}^{k}\max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[(|w_{r}^{\top}x|^{p}+|w_{r}^{*\top}x|^{p})\cdot|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\cdot(x^{\top}a)^{2}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{\max}^{2}L_{1}L_{2}\sum_{r=1}^{k}[(\|w_{r}\|^{p}+\|w_{r}^{*}\|^{p})\|w_{r}-w_{r}^{*}\|]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ kv_{\max}^{2}L_{1}L_{2}\sigma_{1}^{p}(W^{*})\|W-W^{*}\|,

(13)

where the first step follows by
y=\sum_{r=1}^{k}v_{r}^{*}\phi(w_{r}^{*\top}x)
, the second step follows by definition of spectral norm and
v_{r}^{*}v_{i}^{*}\leq|v_{\max}|^{2}
, the third step follows by Property
3.3
(a), i.e.,
|\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)|\leq L_{2}
, the fourth step follows by
|\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-\phi(w_{r}^{*\top}x)\leq\max_{z\in[w_{r}^{\top}x,w_{r}^{*\top}x]}|\phi^{\prime}(z)||(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|
, the fifth step follows Property
3.1
, i.e.,
|\phi^{\prime}(z)|\leq L_{1}|z|^{p}
, the sixth step follows by
\max_{z\in[w_{r}^{\top}x,w_{r}^{*\top}x]}|z|^{p}\leq(|w_{r}^{\top}x|^{p}+|w_{r}^{*\top}x|^{p})
, the seventh step follows by Fact
B.6
.

Putting it all together, we can bound the error by

\displaystyle\penalty\ \|\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W)-\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W^{*})\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}a^{\top}(\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W)-\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W^{*}))a

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sum_{l=1}^{k}a_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{i,l}a_{l}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}a_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{i,i}a_{i}+\sum_{i\neq l}a_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{i,l}a_{l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|\Delta_{i,i}\|\|a_{i}\|^{2}+\sum_{i\neq l}\|\Delta_{i,l}\|\|a_{i}\|\|a_{l}\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}C_{1}\|a_{i}\|^{2}+\sum_{i\neq l}C_{2}\|a_{i}\|\|a_{l}\|\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{\|a\|=1}\left(C_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|a_{i}\|^{2}+C_{2}\left(\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|a_{i}\|\right)^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|a_{i}\|^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\|a\|=1}\left(C_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|a_{i}\|^{2}+C_{2}\left(k\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|a_{i}\|^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{k}\|a_{i}\|^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{\|a\|=1}(C_{1}+C_{2}(k-1))

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ k^{2}v_{\max}^{2}L_{1}L_{2}\sigma_{1}^{p}(W^{*})\|W-W^{*}\|.

where the first step follows by definition of spectral norm and
a
denotes a vector
\in\mathbb{R}^{dk}
, the first inequality follows by
\|A\|=\max_{\|x\|\neq 0,\|y\|\neq 0}\frac{x^{\top}Ay}{\|x\|\|y\|}
, the second inequality follows by
\|\Delta_{i,i}\|\leq C_{1}
and
\|\Delta_{i,l}\|\leq C_{2}
, the third inequality follows by Cauchy-Scharwz inequality, the eighth step follows by
\sum_{i=1}\|a_{i}\|^{2}=1
, and the last step follows by Eq (
D.3.1
) and (
D.3.1
).
∎

D.3.2
Empirical and Population Difference for Smooth Activations

The goal of this Section is to prove Lemma
D.11
. For each
i\in[k]
, let
\sigma_{i}
denote the
i
-th largest singular value of
W^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
.

Note that Bernstein inequality requires the spectral norm of each random matrix to be bounded almost surely.
However, since we assume Gaussian distribution for
x
,
\|x\|^{2}
is not bounded almost surely. The main idea is to do truncation and then use Matrix Bernstein inequality. Details can be found in Lemma
B.7
and Corollary
B.8
.

Lemma D.11

(Empirical and population difference for smooth activations)
.

Let
\phi(z)
satisfy Property
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
(a).
Let
W
satisfy
\|W-W^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{k}/2
. Let
S
denote a set of i.i.d. samples from distribution
{\cal D}
(defined in (
1
)). Then for any
t\geq 1
and
0<\epsilon<1/2
, if

\displaystyle|S|\geq\epsilon^{-2}d\kappa^{2}\tau\cdot\poly(\log d,t)

then we have, with probability at least
1-d^{-\Omega(t)}
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)-\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W)\|\lesssim

\displaystyle v^{2}_{\max}k\sigma_{1}^{p}(\epsilon\sigma_{1}^{p}+\|W-W^{*}\|).

Proof.

Define
\Delta=\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W)-\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)
. Let’s first consider the diagonal blocks.
Define

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}=

\displaystyle\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\overset{k}{\underset{r=1}{\sum}}v_{r}^{*}\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}+v^{*2}_{i}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle\penalty\ -\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\overset{k}{\underset{r=1}{\sum}}v_{r}^{*}\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x_{j})-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x_{j})x_{j}x_{j}^{\top}+v^{*2}_{i}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x_{j})x_{j}x_{j}^{\top}\right).

Let’s further decompose
\Delta_{i,i}
into
\Delta_{i,i}=\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}+\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}
, where

\displaystyle\penalty\ \Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(\overset{k}{\underset{r=1}{\sum}}v_{r}^{*}\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\overset{k}{\underset{r=1}{\sum}}v_{r}^{*}\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x_{j})-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x_{j})x_{j}x_{j}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{i}^{*}\sum_{r=1}^{k}\left(v_{r}^{*}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[(\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-\phi(w_{r}^{*\top}x))\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right]\right.

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left.(\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x_{j})-\phi(w_{r}^{*\top}x_{j}))\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x_{j})x_{j}x_{j}^{\top}\right),

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}=\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[v^{*2}_{i}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}[v^{*2}_{i}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x_{j})x_{j}x_{j}^{\top}].

(14)

The off-diagonal block is

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,l}=v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\left(\underset{x\sim\D_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x_{j})\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x_{j})x_{j}x_{j}^{\top}\right)

Combining Claims.
D.12
,
D.13
and
D.14
, and taking a union bound over
k^{2}
different
\Delta_{i,j}
, we obtain if

n\geq\epsilon^{-2}\kappa^{2}(W^{*})\tau d\cdot\poly(t,\log d)

for any
\epsilon\in(0,1/2)
, with probability at least
1-1/d^{t}
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)-\nabla^{2}f(W)\|\lesssim v^{2}_{\max}k\sigma_{1}^{p}(W^{*})(\epsilon\sigma_{1}^{p}(W^{*})+\|W-W^{*}\|)

∎

Claim D.12
.

For each
i\in[k]
, if
n\geq d\poly(\log d,t)

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}\|\lesssim kv_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{p}(W^{*})\|W-W^{*}\|

holds with probability
1-1/d^{4t}
.

Proof.

For each
r\in[k]
, we define function
\widehat{B}_{r}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
,

\displaystyle\widehat{B}_{r}(x)=L_{1}L_{2}\cdot(|w_{r}^{\top}x|^{p}+|w_{r}^{*\top}x|^{p})\cdot|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\cdot xx^{\top}.

According to Properties
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
(a), we have for each
x\in S
,

-\widehat{B}_{r}(x)\preceq(\phi(w_{r}^{\top}x)-\phi(w_{r}^{*\top}x))\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\preceq\widehat{B}_{r}(x)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}\preceq v_{\max}^{2}\sum_{r=1}^{k}\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[\widehat{B}_{r}(x)]+\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\widehat{B}_{r}(x)\right).

Let
h_{r}(x)=L_{1}L_{2}|w_{r}^{\top}x|^{p}\cdot|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|
. Let
\overline{B}_{r}=\E_{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}[h_{r}(x)xx^{\top}]
. Define function
B_{r}(x)=h_{r}(x)xx^{\top}
.

(I) Bounding
|h_{r}(x)|
.

According to Fact
B.1
, we have for any constant
t\geq 1
,
with probability
1-1/(nd^{4t})
,

\displaystyle|h_{r}(x)|=L_{1}L_{2}|w_{r}^{\top}x|^{p}|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\lesssim L_{1}L_{2}\|w_{r}\|^{p}\|w_{r}-w_{r}^{*}\|(t\log n)^{(p+1)/2}.

(II) Bounding
\|\overline{B}_{r}\|
.

\displaystyle\|\overline{B}_{r}\|

\displaystyle\geq\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[L_{1}L_{2}|w_{r}^{\top}x|^{p}|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\left(\frac{(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x}{\|w_{r}-w_{r}^{*}\|}\right)^{2}\right]\gtrsim L_{1}L_{2}\|w_{r}\|^{p}\|w_{r}-w_{r}^{*}\|,

where the first step follows by definition of spectral norm, and last step follows by Fact
B.6
. Using Fact
B.6
, we can also prove an upper bound
\|\overline{B}_{r}\|
,
\|\overline{B}_{r}\|\lesssim L_{1}L_{2}\|w_{r}\|^{p}\|w_{r}-w_{r}^{*}\|
.

(III) Bounding
(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h^{4}(x)])^{1/4}

Using Fact
B.6
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[h^{4}(x)]\right)^{1/4}=L_{1}L_{2}\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\left(|w_{r}^{\top}x|^{p}|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\right)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/4}\lesssim L_{1}L_{2}\|w_{r}\|^{p}\|w_{r}-w_{r}^{*}\|.

By applying Corollary
B.8
, if
n\geq\epsilon^{-2}d\poly(\log d,t)
, then with probability
1-1/d^{4t}
,

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left\|\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[|w_{r}^{\top}x|^{p}\cdot|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\cdot xx^{\top}\right]-\frac{1}{|S|}\left(\sum_{x\in S}|w_{r}^{\top}x_{j}|^{p}\cdot|(w_{r}-w_{r}^{*})^{\top}x|\cdot xx^{\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left\|\overline{B}_{r}-\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}B_{r}(x)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \epsilon\|\overline{B}_{r}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ \epsilon\|w_{r}\|^{p}\|w_{r}-w_{r}^{*}\|.

(15)

If
\epsilon\leq 1/2
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{k}v_{\max}^{2}\|\overline{B}_{r}\|\lesssim kv_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{p}(W^{*})\|W-W^{*}\|

∎

Claim D.13
.

For each
i\in[k]
, if
n\geq\epsilon^{-2}d\tau\poly(\log d,t)
, then

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}\|\lesssim\epsilon v_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}

holds with probability
1-1/d^{4t}
.

Proof.

Recall the definition of
\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}
.

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}=\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[v^{*2}_{i}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]-\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}[v^{*2}_{i}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]

Let
h_{i}(x)=\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)
. Let
\overline{B}_{i}=\E_{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}[h_{i}(x)xx^{\top}]
Define function
B_{i}(x)=h_{i}(x)xx^{\top}
.

(I) Bounding
|h_{i}(x)|
.

For any constant
t\geq 1
,
(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x))^{2}\leq L_{1}^{2}|w_{i}^{\top}x|^{2p}\lesssim L_{1}^{2}\|w_{i}\|^{2p}t^{p}\log^{p}(n)
with probability
1-1/(nd^{4t})
according to Fact
B.1
.

(II) Bounding
\|\overline{B}_{i}\|
.

\displaystyle\left\|\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]\right\|=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\left(\alpha\overline{w}_{i}^{\top}x+\beta x^{\top}v\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}=1,\|v\|=1}\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}\left[\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\left((\alpha\overline{w}_{i}^{\top}x)^{2}+(\beta x^{\top}v)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}=1}\left(\alpha^{2}\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime 2}(\|w_{i}\|z)z^{2}]+\beta^{2}\underset{z\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime 2}(\|w_{i}\|z)]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime 2}(\|w_{i}\|z)z^{2}],\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime 2}(\|w_{i}\|z)]\right)

where
\overline{w}_{i}=w_{i}/\|w_{i}\|
and
v
is a unit vector orthogonal to
w_{i}
such that
a=\alpha\overline{w}_{i}+\beta v
. Now from Property
3.2
, we have

\displaystyle\rho(\|w_{i}\|)\leq\left\|\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]\right\|\lesssim L_{1}^{2}\|w_{i}\|^{2p}.

(III) Bounding
(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h_{i}^{4}(x)])^{1/4}
.

\displaystyle\left(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h_{i}^{4}(x)]\right)^{1/4}=\left(\underset{x\sim{\cal D}_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime 8}(w_{i}^{\top}x)]\right)^{1/4}\lesssim L_{1}^{2}\|w_{i}\|^{2p}.

By applying Corollary
B.8
, we have, for any
0<\epsilon<1
, if
n\geq\epsilon^{-2}d\frac{\|w_{i}\|^{4p}}{\rho^{2}(\|w_{i}\|)}\poly(\log d,t)

the following bound holds

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{B}_{i}-\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}B_{i}(x)\right\|\leq\epsilon\|\overline{B}_{i}\|,

with probability at least
1-1/d^{4t}
.

Therefore, if

n\geq\epsilon^{-2}d\tau\poly(\log d,t)
,
where
\tau=\frac{(3\sigma_{1}/2)^{4p}}{\min_{\sigma\in[\sigma_{k}/2,3\sigma_{1}/2]}\rho^{2}(\sigma)}
, we have with probability
1-1/d^{4t}

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}\|\lesssim\epsilon v^{2}_{\max}\sigma_{1}^{2p}

∎

Claim D.14
.

For each
i\in[k],j\in[k],i\neq j
, if
n\geq\epsilon^{-2}\kappa^{2}\tau d\poly(\log d,t)
, then

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,j}\|\leq\epsilon v_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}(W^{*})

holds with probability
1-1/d^{4t}
.

Proof.

Recall the definition of off-diagonal blocks
\Delta_{i,l}
,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,l}=v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\left(\underset{x\sim\D_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]-\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right)

(16)

Let
h_{i,l}(x)=\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)
. Define function
B_{i,l}(x)=h_{i,l}(x)xx^{\top}
. Let
\overline{B}_{i,l}=\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h_{i,l}(x)xx^{\top}]
.

(I) Bounding
|h_{i,l}(x)|
.

For any constant
t\geq 1
, we have with probability
1-1/(nd^{4t})

\displaystyle|h_{i,l}(x)|=

\displaystyle\penalty\ |\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ L_{1}^{2}\|w_{i}^{\top}x\|^{p}\|w_{l}^{\top}x\|^{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ L_{1}^{2}\|w_{i}\|^{p}\|w_{l}\|^{p}(t\log n)^{p}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ L_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}(t\log n)^{p}

where the third step follows by Fact
B.1
.

(II) Bounding
\|\overline{B}_{i,l}\|
.

Let
U\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times 2}
be the orthogonal basis of
\text{span}\{w_{i},w_{l}\}
and
U_{\perp}\in\R^{d\times(d-2)}
be the complementary matrix of
U
. Let matrix
V\in\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2}
denote
U^{\top}[w_{i}\;w_{l}]
, then
UV=[w_{i}\;w_{l}]\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times 2}
. Given any vector
a\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, there exist vectors
b\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
c\in\mathbb{R}^{d-2}
such that
a=Ub+U_{\perp}c
. We can simplify
\|\overline{B}_{i,l}\|
in the following way,

\displaystyle\|\overline{B}_{i,l}\|=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left\|\underset{x\sim\D_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}\underset{x\sim\D_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)(x^{\top}a)^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|b\|^{2}+\|c\|^{2}=1}\underset{x\sim\D_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)(b^{\top}U^{\top}x+c^{\top}U_{\perp}^{\top}x)^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|b\|^{2}+\|c\|^{2}=1}\underset{x\sim\D_{d}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)((b^{\top}U^{\top}x)^{2}+(c^{\top}U_{\perp}^{\top}x)^{2})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|b\|^{2}+\|c\|^{2}=1}\left(\underbrace{\underset{z\sim\D_{2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)(b^{\top}z)^{2}]}_{A_{1}}+\underbrace{\underset{z\sim\D_{2},s\sim\D_{d-2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)(c^{\top}s)^{2}]}_{A_{2}}\right)

We can lower bound the term
A_{1}
,

\displaystyle A_{1}=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{z\sim\D_{2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)(b^{\top}z)^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(2\pi)^{-1}\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)(b^{\top}z)^{2}e^{-\|z\|^{2}/2}dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(2\pi)^{-1}\phi^{\prime}(s_{1})\phi^{\prime}(s_{2})(b^{\top}V^{\dagger\top}s)^{2}e^{-\|V^{\dagger\top}s\|^{2}/2}\cdot|\det(V^{\dagger})|ds

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(2\pi)^{-1}(\phi^{\prime}(s_{1})\phi^{\prime}(s_{2}))(b^{\top}V^{\dagger\top}s)^{2}e^{-\sigma^{2}_{1}(V^{\dagger})\|s\|^{2}/2}\cdot|\det(V^{\dagger})|ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(2\pi)^{-1}(\phi^{\prime}(u_{1}/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger}))\phi^{\prime}(u_{2}/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger})))\cdot(b^{\top}V^{\dagger\top}u/\sigma_{1}(V^{\dagger}))^{2}e^{-\|u\|^{2}/2}|\det(V^{\dagger})|/\sigma_{1}^{2}(V^{\dagger})du

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{\sigma_{2}(V)}{\sigma_{1}(V)}\underset{u\sim\D_{2}}{\E}\left[(p^{\top}u)^{2}\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot u_{1})\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot u_{2})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{\sigma_{2}(V)}{\sigma_{1}(V)}\underset{u\sim\D_{2}}{\E}\left[\left((p_{1}u_{1})^{2}+(p_{2}u_{2})^{2}+2p_{1}p_{2}u_{1}u_{2}\right)\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot u_{1})\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot u_{2})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{\sigma_{2}(V)}{\sigma_{1}(V)}\left(\|p\|^{2}\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)z^{2}]\cdot\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)]\right.

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left.((p^{\top}{\mathbf{1}})^{2}-\|p\|^{2})\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)z]^{2}\right)

where
p=V^{\dagger}b\cdot\sigma_{2}(V)\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
.

Since we are maximizing over
b\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we can choose
b
such that
\|p\|=\|b\|
. Then

\displaystyle A_{1}=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{z\sim\D_{2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)(b^{\top}z)^{2}]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{\sigma_{2}(V)}{\sigma_{1}(V)}\|b\|^{2}\left(\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)z^{2}]\cdot\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)]-\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)z]^{2}\right)

For the term
A_{2}
, similarly we have

\displaystyle A_{2}=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{z\sim\D_{2},s\sim\D_{d-2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)(c^{\top}s)^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \underset{z\sim\D_{2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)]\underset{s\sim\D_{d-2}}{\E}[(c^{\top}s)^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \|c\|^{2}\underset{z\sim\D_{2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \|c\|^{2}\frac{\sigma_{2}(V)}{\sigma_{1}(V)}\left(\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)]\right)^{2}

For simplicity, we just set
\|b\|=1
and
\|c\|=0
to lower bound,

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left\|\underset{x\sim\D_{d}}{\E}\left[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{\sigma_{2}(V)}{\sigma_{1}(V)}\left(\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)z^{2}]\cdot\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)]-\left(\underset{z\sim\D_{1}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(\sigma_{2}(V)\cdot z)z]\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{\sigma_{2}(V)}{\sigma_{1}(V)}\rho(\sigma_{2}(V))

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{1}{\kappa(W^{*})}\rho(\sigma_{2}(V))

where the second step is from Property
3.2
and the fact that
\sigma_{k}/2\leq\sigma_{2}(V)\leq\sigma_{1}(V)\leq 3\sigma_{1}/2
.

For the upper bound of
\|\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]\|
,
we have

\displaystyle\underset{z\sim\D_{2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)(b^{\top}z)^{2}]\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ L_{1}^{2}\underset{z\sim\D_{2}}{\E}[|v_{1}^{\top}z|^{p}\cdot|v_{2}^{\top}z|^{p}\cdot|b^{\top}z|^{2}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ L_{1}^{2}\|v_{1}\|^{p}\|v_{2}\|^{p}\|b\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ L_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}

where the first step follows by Property
3.1
, the second step follows by Fact
B.6
, and the last step follows by
\|v_{1}\|\leq\sigma_{1}
,
\|v_{2}\|\leq\sigma_{1}
and
\|b\|\leq 1
.
Similarly, we can upper bound,

\displaystyle\underset{z\sim\D_{2},s\sim\D_{d-2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)(c^{\top}s)^{2}]=\|c\|^{2}\underset{z\sim\D_{2}}{\E}[\phi^{\prime}(v_{1}^{\top}z)\phi^{\prime}(v_{2}^{\top}z)]\lesssim L_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}]\right\|\lesssim L_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}\lesssim\sigma_{1}^{2p}.

(III) Bounding
(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h_{i,l}^{4}(x)])^{1/4}
.

\displaystyle\left(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h_{i,l}^{4}(x)]\right)^{1/4}=\left(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[\phi^{\prime 4}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\cdot\phi^{\prime 4}(w_{l}^{\top}x)]\right)^{1/4}\lesssim L_{1}^{2}\|w_{i}\|^{p}\|w_{l}\|^{p}\lesssim L_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}.

Therefore, applying Corollary
B.8
, we have, if

n\geq\epsilon^{-2}\kappa^{2}(W^{*})\tau d\poly(\log d,t)
, then

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,j}\|\penalty\ \leq\penalty\ \epsilon v^{2}_{\max}\sigma_{1}^{2p}(W^{*}).

holds with probability at least
1-1/d^{4t}
.
∎

D.4
Error Bound of Hessians near the Ground Truth for Non-smooth Activations

The goal of this Section is to prove Lemma
D.15
,

Lemma D.15

(Error bound of Hessians near the ground truth for non-smooth activations)
.

Let
\phi(z)
satisfy Property
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
(b). Let
W
satisfy
\|W-W^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{k}/2
. Let
S
denote a set of
i.i.d. samples from the distribution defined in (
1
). Then for any
t\geq 1
and
0<\epsilon<1/2
, if

\displaystyle|S|\geq\epsilon^{-2}\kappa^{2}\tau d\poly(\log d,t)

with probability at least
1-d^{-\Omega(t)}
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)-\nabla^{2}f_{\cal D}(W^{*})\|\lesssim v^{2}_{\max}k\sigma_{1}^{2p}(\epsilon+(\sigma_{k}^{-1}\cdot\|W-W^{*}\|)^{1/2}).

Proof.

As we noted previously, when Property
3.3
(b) holds, the diagonal blocks of the empirical Hessian can be written as, with probability
1
,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)}{\partial w_{i}^{2}}=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{(x,y)\in S}(v^{*}_{i}\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x))^{2}xx^{\top}

We construct a matrix
H\in\R^{dk\times dk}
with
i,l
-th block as

\displaystyle H_{i,l}=v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right]\quad\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d},\quad\forall i\in[k],l\in[k].

Note that
H\neq\nabla^{2}f_{\D}(W)
. However we can still bound
\|H-\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)\|
and
\|H-\nabla^{2}f_{\D}(W^{*})\|
when we have enough samples and
\|W-W^{*}\|
is small enough. The proof for
\|H-\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)\|
basically follows the proof of Lemma
D.11
as
\Delta_{ii}^{(2)}
in Eq. (
14
) and
\Delta_{il}
in Eq. (
16
) forms the blocks of
H-\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)
and we can bound them without smoothness of
\phi(\cdot)
.

Now we focus on
H-\nabla^{2}f_{\D}(W^{*})
. We again consider each block.

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,l}=\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x))xx^{\top}\right].

We used the boundedness of
\phi^{\prime\prime}(z)
to prove Lemma
D.10
. Here we can’t use this condition. Without smoothness, we will stop at the following position.

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left\|\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x))xx^{\top}]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ |v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}|\max_{\|a\|=1}\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ |v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}|\max_{\|a\|=1}\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|\right.

\displaystyle\penalty\ +\left.|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ |v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}|\max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\penalty\ +\left.\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{*\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]\right).

(17)

where the first step follows by definition of spectral norm, the second step follows by triangle inequality, and the last step follows by linearity of expectation.

Without loss of generality, we just bound the first term in the above formulation. Let
U
be the orthogonal basis of
\text{span}(w_{i},w_{i}^{*},w_{l})
. If
w_{i},w_{i}^{*},w_{l}
are independent,
U
is
d
-by-
3
. Otherwise it can be
d
-by-
\mathrm{rank}(\text{span}(w_{i},w_{i}^{*},w_{l}))
. Without loss of generality, we assume
U=\text{span}(w_{i},w_{i}^{*},w_{l})
is
d
-by-3. Let
[v_{i}\;v_{i}^{*}\;v_{l}]=U^{\top}[w_{i}\;w_{i}^{*}\;w_{l}]\in\mathbb{R}^{3\times 3}
, and
[u_{i}\;u_{i}^{*}\;u_{l}]=U_{\bot}^{\top}[w_{i}\;w_{i}^{*}\;w_{l}]\in\mathbb{R}^{(d-3)\times 3}
. Let
a=Ub+U_{\perp}c
, where
U_{\perp}\in\R^{d\times(d-3)}
is the complementary matrix of
U
.

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|(x^{\top}(Ub+U_{\bot}c))^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|\left((x^{\top}Ub)^{2}+(x^{\top}U_{\perp}c)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|(x^{\top}Ub)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{*\top}x)||\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)|(x^{\top}U_{\perp}c)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)-\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)||\phi^{\prime}(v_{l}^{\top}z)|(z^{\top}b)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{y\sim\D_{d-3}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(u_{i}^{\top}y)-\phi^{\prime}(u_{i}^{*\top}y)||\phi^{\prime}(u_{l}^{\top}y)|(y^{\top}c)^{2}\right]

(18)

where the first step follows by
a=Ub+U_{\bot}c
, the last step follows by
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
.

By Property
3.3
(b), we have
e
exceptional points which have
\phi^{\prime\prime}(z)\neq 0
. Let these
e
points be
p_{1},p_{2},\cdots,p_{e}
. Note that if
v_{i}^{\top}z
and
v_{i}^{*\top}z
are not separated by any of these exceptional points, i.e., there exists no
j\in[e]
such that
v_{i}^{\top}z\leq p_{j}\leq v_{i}^{*\top}z
or
v_{i}^{*\top}z\leq p_{j}\leq v_{i}^{\top}z
, then we have
\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)=\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)
since
\phi^{\prime\prime}(s)
are zeros except for
\{p_{j}\}_{j=1,2,\cdots,e}
. So we consider the probability that
v_{i}^{\top}z,v_{i}^{*\top}z
are separated by any exception point. We use
\xi_{j}
to denote the event that
v_{i}^{\top}z,v_{i}^{*\top}z
are separated by an exceptional point
p_{j}
. By union bound,
1-\sum_{j=1}^{e}\Pr[\xi_{j}]
is the probability that
v_{i}^{\top}z,v_{i}^{*\top}z
are not separated by any exceptional point.
The first term of Equation (
D.4
) can be bounded as,

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[|\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)-\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)||\phi^{\prime}(v_{l}^{\top}z)|(z^{\top}b)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[{\mathbf{1}}_{\cup_{j=1}^{e}\xi_{j}}|\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)+\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)||\phi^{\prime}(v_{l}^{\top}z)|(z^{\top}b)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left(\E_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[{\mathbf{1}}_{\cup_{j=1}^{e}\xi_{j}}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\E_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[(\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)+\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z))^{2}\phi^{\prime}(v_{l}^{\top}z)^{2}(z^{\top}b)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left(\sum_{j=1}^{e}\Pr_{z\sim\D_{3}}[\xi_{j}]\right)^{1/2}\left(\E_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[(\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)+\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z))^{2}\phi^{\prime}(v_{l}^{\top}z)^{2}(z^{\top}b)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ \left(\sum_{j=1}^{e}\Pr_{z\sim\D_{3}}[\xi_{j}]\right)^{1/2}(\|v_{i}\|^{p}+\|v_{i}^{*}\|^{p})\|v_{l}\|^{p}\|b\|^{2}

where the first step follows by if
v_{i}^{\top}z,v_{i}^{*\top}z
are not separated by any exceptional point then
\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)=\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)
and the last step follows by Hölder’s inequality and Property
3.1
.

It remains to upper bound
\Pr_{z\sim\D_{3}}[\xi_{j}]
. First note that if
v_{i}^{\top}z,v_{i}^{*\top}z
are separated by an exceptional point,
p_{j}
, then
|v_{i}^{*\top}z-p_{j}|\leq|v_{i}^{\top}z-v_{i}^{*\top}z|\leq\|v_{i}-v_{i}^{*}\|\|z\|
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\Pr_{z\sim\D_{3}}[\xi_{j}]\leq\Pr_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[\frac{|v_{i}^{\top}z-p_{j}|}{\|z\|}\leq\|v_{i}-v_{i}^{*}\|\right].

Note that
(\frac{v_{i}^{*\top}z}{\|z\|\|v_{i}^{*}\|}+1)/2
follows Beta(1,1) distribution which is uniform distribution on
[0,1]
.

\displaystyle\penalty\ \Pr_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[\frac{|v_{i}^{*\top}z-p_{j}|}{\|z\|\|v_{i}^{*}\|}\leq\frac{\|v_{i}-v_{i}^{*}\|}{\|v_{i}^{*}\|}\right]\leq\Pr_{z\sim\D_{3}}\left[\frac{|v_{i}^{*\top}z|}{\|z\|\|v_{i}^{*}\|}\leq\frac{\|v_{i}-v_{i}^{*}\|}{\|v_{i}^{*}\|}\right]\lesssim\frac{\|v_{i}-v_{i}^{*}\|}{\|v_{i}^{*}\|}\lesssim\frac{\|W-W^{*}\|}{\sigma_{k}(W^{*})},

where the first step is because we can view
\frac{v_{i}^{*\top}z}{\|z\|}
and
\frac{p_{j}}{\|z\|}
as two independent random variables: the former is about the direction of
z
and the later is related to the magnitude of
z
.
Thus, we have

\displaystyle\E_{z\in\D_{3}}[|\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{\top}z)-\phi^{\prime}(v_{i}^{*\top}z)||\phi^{\prime}(v_{l}^{\top}z)|(z^{\top}b)^{2}]\lesssim(e\|W-W^{*}\|/\sigma_{k}(W^{*}))^{1/2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}(W^{*})\|b\|^{2}.

(19)

Similarly we have

\displaystyle\E_{y\in\D_{d-3}}[|\phi^{\prime}(u_{i}^{\top}y)-\phi^{\prime}(u_{i}^{*\top}y)||\phi^{\prime}(u_{l}^{\top}y)|(y^{\top}c)^{2}]\lesssim(e\|W-W^{*}\|/\sigma_{k}(W^{*}))^{1/2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}(W^{*})\|c\|^{2}.

(20)

Finally combining Eq. (
17
), Eq. (
19
) and Eq. (
20
), we have

\|H-\nabla^{2}f_{\D}(W^{*})\|\lesssim kv_{\max}^{2}(e\|W-W^{*}\|/\sigma_{k}(W^{*}))^{1/2}\sigma_{1}^{2p}(W^{*}),

which completes the proof.

∎

D.5
Positive Definiteness for a Small Region

Here we introduce a lemma which shows that the Hessian of any
W
, which may be dependent on the samples but is very close to an anchor point, is close to the Hessian of this anchor point.

Lemma D.16
.

Let
S
denote a set of samples from Distribution
{\cal D}
defined in Eq. (
1
). Let
W^{a}\in\R^{d\times k}
be a point (respect to function
\widehat{f}_{S}(W)
), which is independent of the samples
S
, satisfying
\|W^{a}-W^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{k}/2
. Assume
\phi
satisfies Property
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
(a). Then for any
t\geq 1
, if

\displaystyle|S|\geq d\poly(\log d,t),

with probability at least
1-d^{-t}
, for any
W
(which is not necessarily to be independent of samples) satisfying
\|W^{a}-W\|\leq\sigma_{k}/4
, we have

\|\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)-\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W^{a})\|\leq kv^{2}_{\max}\sigma_{1}^{p}(\|W^{a}-W^{*}\|+\|W-W^{a}\|d^{(p+1)/2}).

Proof.

Let
\Delta=\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W)-\nabla^{2}\widehat{f}_{S}(W^{a})\in\mathbb{R}^{dk\times dk}
, then
\Delta
can be thought of as
k^{2}
blocks, and each block has size
d\times d
.
The off-diagonal blocks are,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,l}=v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{a\top}x)\right)xx^{\top}

For diagonal blocks,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\left(\overset{k}{\underset{q=1}{\sum}}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{\top}x)-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}+v^{*2}_{i}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\left(\overset{k}{\underset{q=1}{\sum}}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)-y\right)v_{i}^{*}\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)xx^{\top}+v^{*2}_{i}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)xx^{\top}\right)

We further decompose
\Delta_{i,i}
into
\Delta_{i,i}=\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}+\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}
, where

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}=v_{i}^{*}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\left(\overset{k}{\underset{q=1}{\sum}}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{\top}x)-y\right)\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\left(\overset{k}{\underset{q=1}{\sum}}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)-y\right)\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)\right)xx^{\top},

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}=

\displaystyle v^{*2}_{i}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{(x,y)\in S}\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}-\phi^{\prime 2}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)xx^{\top}.

(21)

We can further decompose
\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}
into
\Delta_{i,i}^{(1,1)}
and
\Delta_{i,i}^{(1,2)}
,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{i}^{*}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\overset{k}{\underset{q=1}{\sum}}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{\top}x)-y\right)\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\left(\sum_{q=1}^{k}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)-y\right)\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)xx^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{i}^{*}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\sum_{q=1}^{k}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{\top}x)-\overset{k}{{\underset{q=1}{\sum}}}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)\right)\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{i}^{*}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\sum_{q=1}^{k}v_{q}^{*}\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)-y\right)(\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x))xx^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{i}^{*}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\sum_{q=1}^{k}v_{q}^{*}(\phi(w_{q}^{\top}x)-\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x))\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)xx^{\top}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\penalty\ v_{i}^{*}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\sum_{q=1}^{k}v_{q}^{*}(\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)-\phi(w_{q}^{*\top}x))(\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x))xx^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \Delta_{i,i}^{(1,1)}+\Delta_{i,i}^{(1,2)}.

Combining Claim
D.17
and Claim
D.18
, we have if

\displaystyle n\geq d\poly(\log d,t)

with probability at least
1-1/d^{4t}
,

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}\|\lesssim kv^{2}_{\max}\sigma_{1}^{p}(\|W^{a}-W^{*}\|+\|W^{a}-W\|d^{(p+1)/2}).

(22)

Therefore, combining Eq. (
22
), Claim
D.19
and Claim
D.20
, we complete the proof.
∎

Claim D.17
.

For each
i\in[k]
, if
n\geq d\poly(\log d,t)
, then

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(1,1)}\|\lesssim kv_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{p}\|W^{a}-W\|d^{(p+1)/2}

Proof.

Define function
h_{1}(x)=\|x\|^{p+1}
and
h_{2}(x)=|w_{q}^{*\top}x|^{p}|(w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a})^{\top}x|
. Note that
h_{1}
and
h_{2}
don’t contain
W
which maybe depend on the samples. Therefore, we can use the modified matrix Bernstein inequality (Corollary
B.8
) to bound
\Delta_{i,i}^{(1)}
.

(I) Bounding
|h_{1}(x)|
.

By Fact
B.2
, we have
h_{1}(x)\lesssim(td\log n)^{(p+1)/2}
with probability at least
1-1/(nd^{4t})
.

(II) Bounding
\|\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[\|x\|^{p+1}xx^{\top}]\|
.

Let
g(x)=(2\pi)^{-d/2}e^{-\|x\|^{2}/2}
. Note that
xg(x)\mathrm{d}x=-\mathrm{d}g(x)
.

\displaystyle\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[\|x\|^{p+1}xx^{\top}\right]=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int\|x\|^{p+1}g(x)xx^{\top}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ -\int\|x\|^{p+1}\mathrm{d}(g(x))x^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ -\int\|x\|^{p+1}\mathrm{d}(g(x)x^{\top})+\int\|x\|^{p+1}g(x)I_{d}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int d(\|x\|^{p+1})g(x)x^{\top}+\int\|x\|^{p+1}g(x)I_{d}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int(p+1)\|x\|^{p-1}g(x)xx^{\top}\mathrm{d}x+\int\|x\|^{p+1}g(x)I_{d}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\succeq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \int\|x\|^{p+1}g(x)I_{d}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ \E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[\|x\|^{p+1}]I_{d}.

Since
\|x\|^{2}
follows
\chi^{2}
distribution with degree
d
,
\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[\|x\|^{q}]=2^{q/2}\frac{\Gamma((d+q)/2)}{\Gamma(d/2)}
for any
q\geq 0
. So,
d^{q/2}\lesssim\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[\|x\|^{q}]\lesssim d^{q/2}
. Hence,
\|\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h_{1}(x)xx^{\top}]\|\gtrsim d^{(p+1)/2}
. Also

\displaystyle\left\|\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[h_{1}(x)xx^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[h_{1}(x)(x^{\top}a)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ \max_{\|a\|=1}\left(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[h_{1}^{2}(x)\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[(x^{\top}a)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\penalty\ d^{(p+1)/2}.

(III) Bounding
(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h^{4}_{1}(x)])^{1/4}
.

\displaystyle\left(\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h^{4}_{1}(x)]\right)^{1/4}\lesssim d^{(p+1)/2}.

Define function
B(x)=h(x)xx^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
,
\forall i\in[n]
. Let
\overline{B}=\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}[h(x)xx^{\top}]
.
Therefore by applying Corollary
B.8
, we obtain for any
0<\epsilon<1
, if

\displaystyle n\geq\epsilon^{-2}d\poly(\log d,t)

with probability at least
1-1/d^{t}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\|x\|^{p+1}xx^{\top}-\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[\|x\|^{p+1}xx^{\top}\right]\right\|\lesssim\delta d^{(p+1)/2}.

Therefore we have with probability at least
1-1/d^{t}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\|x\|^{p+1}xx^{\top}\right\|\lesssim d^{(p+1)/2}.

(23)

∎

Claim D.18
.

For each
i\in[k]
, if
n\geq d\poly(\log d,t)
, then

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(1,2)}\|\lesssim k\sigma_{1}^{p}\|W^{a}-W^{*}\|,

holds with probability at least
1-1/d^{4t}
.

Proof.

Recall the definition of
\Delta_{i,i}^{(1,2)}
,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,i}^{(1,2)}=v_{i}^{*}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\sum_{q=1}^{k}v_{q}^{*}(\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)-\phi(w_{q}^{*\top}x))(\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x))xx^{\top}

In order to upper bound the
\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(1,2)}\|
, it suffices to upper bound the spectral norm of this quantity,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\sim S}(\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)-\phi(w_{q}^{*\top}x))(\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x))xx^{\top}.

By Property
3.1
, we have

\displaystyle|\phi(w_{q}^{a\top}x)-\phi(w_{q}^{*\top}x)|\lesssim L_{1}(|w_{q}^{a\top}x|^{p}+|w_{q}^{*\top}x|)|(w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a})^{\top}x|.

By Property
3.3
, we have
|\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)|\leq 2L_{2}
.

For the second part
|w_{q}^{*\top}x|^{p}|(w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a})^{\top}x|xx^{\top}
, according to Eq. (
D.3.2
), we have with probability
1-d^{-t}
, if
n\geq d\poly(\log d,t)
,

\displaystyle\left\|\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|w_{q}^{*\top}x|^{p}|(w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a})^{\top}x|xx^{\top}\right]-\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}|w_{q}^{*\top}x|^{p}|(w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a})^{\top}x|xx^{\top}\right\|\lesssim\delta\|w_{q}^{*}\|^{p}\|w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a}\|.

Also, note that

\displaystyle\left\|\E_{x\sim\D_{d}}\left[|w_{q}^{*\top}x|^{p}|(w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a})^{\top}x|xx^{\top}\right]\right\|\lesssim\|w_{q}^{*}\|^{p}\|w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a}\|.

Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}|w_{q}^{*\top}x|^{p}|(w_{q}^{*}-w_{q}^{a})^{\top}x|xx^{\top}\right\|\lesssim\|W^{a}-W^{*}\|\sigma_{1}^{p}.

(24)

∎

Claim D.19
.

For each
i\in[k]
, if
n\geq d\poly(\log d,t)
, then

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}\|\lesssim kv^{2}_{\max}\sigma_{1}^{p}\|W-W^{a}\|d^{(p+1)/2}

holds with probability
1-1/d^{4t}
.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,i}^{(2)}\|\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v^{*2}_{i}\left\|\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left((\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x_{j})-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x))\cdot(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)+\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x))\right)xx^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v^{*2}_{i}\left\|\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(L_{2}|(w_{i}-w_{i}^{a})^{\top}x|\cdot L_{1}(|w_{i}^{\top}x|^{p}+|w_{i}^{a\top}x|^{p})\right)xx^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\penalty\ v^{*2}_{i}\|W-W^{a}\|\left\|\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(L_{2}\|x\|\cdot L_{1}(\|w_{i}\|^{p}\|x\|^{p}+|w_{i}^{a\top}x|^{p})\right)xx^{\top}\right\|.

Applying Corollary
B.8
finishes the proof.

∎

Claim D.20
.

For each
i\in[k],j\in[k],i\neq j
, if
n\geq d\poly(\log d,t)
, then

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i,l}\|\lesssim v_{\max}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{p}\|W^{a}-W\|

holds with probability
1-d^{4t}
.

Proof.

Recall the definition of
\Delta_{i,l}
,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,l}=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{a\top}x)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{a\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{a\top}x)\right)xx^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\penalty\ v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{a\top}x)\right)

\displaystyle\penalty\ +v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{a\top}x)-\phi^{\prime}(w_{i}^{a\top}x)\phi^{\prime}(w_{l}^{a\top}x)\right)xx^{\top}

\displaystyle\preceq

\displaystyle\penalty\ |v^{*}_{i}v^{*}_{l}|\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{x\in S}\left(L_{1}\|w_{i}\|^{p}L_{2}\|w_{l}-w_{l}^{a}\|\|x\|^{p+1}+L_{2}\|w_{i}-w_{i}^{a}\|\|x\|L_{1}\|w_{l}^{a\top}x\|^{p}\right)xx^{\top}

Applying Corollary
B.8
completes the proof.
∎

Appendix E
Tensor Methods

E.1
Tensor Initialization Algorithm

We describe the details of each procedure in Algorithm
1
in this section.

a) Compute the subspace estimation from
\widehat{P}_{2}
(Algorithm
3
)
. Note that the eigenvalues of
P_{2}
and
\widehat{P}_{2}
are not necessarily nonnegative. However, only
k
of the eigenvalues will have large magnitude. So we can first compute the top-k eigenvectors/eigenvalues of both
C\cdot I+\widehat{P}_{2}
and
C\cdot I-\widehat{P}_{2}
, where
C
is large enough such that
C\geq 2\|P_{2}\|
. Then from the
2k
eigen-pairs, we pick the top-
k
eigenvectors with the largest eigenvalues in magnitude, which is executed in
TopK
in Algorithm
3
. For the outputs of
TopK
,
k_{1},k_{2}
are the numbers of picked largest eigenvalues from
C\cdot I+\widehat{P}_{2}
and
C\cdot I-\widehat{P}_{2}
respectively and
\pi_{1}(i)
returns the original index of
i
-th largest eigenvalue from
C\cdot I+\widehat{P}_{2}
and similarly
\pi_{2}
is for
C\cdot I-\widehat{P}_{2}
. Finally orthogonalizing the picked eigenvectors leads to an estimation of the subspace spanned by
\{w_{1}^{*}\;w_{2}^{*}\;\cdots\;w_{k}^{*}\}
. Also note that forming
\widehat{P}_{2}
takes
O(n\cdot d^{2})
time and each step of the power method doing a multiplication between a
d\times d
matrix and a
d\times k
matrix takes
k\cdot d^{2}
time by a naive implementation. Here we reduce this complexity from
O((k+n)d^{2})
to
O(knd)
. The idea is to compute each step of the power method without explicitly forming
\widehat{P}_{2}
. We take
P_{2}=M_{2}
as an example; other cases are similar. In Algorithm
3
, let the step
\widehat{P}_{2}V
be calculated as
\widehat{P}_{2}V=\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{(x,y)\in S}y(x(x^{\top}V)-V)
.
Now each iteration only needs
O(knd)
time. Furthermore,
the number of iterations required will be a small number, since the power
method has a linear convergence rate and as an initialization method we don’t need a very accurate
solution. The detailed algorithm is shown in Algorithm
3
. The approximation error bound of
\widehat{P}_{2}
to
P_{2}
is provided in Lemma
E.5
. Lemma
E.6
provides the theoretical bound for Algorithm
3
.

b) Form and decompose the 3rd-moment
\widehat{R}_{3}
(Algorithm 1 in
[
KCL15
]
)
. We apply the non-orthogonal tensor factorization algorithm, Algorithm 1 in
[
KCL15
]
, to decompose
\widehat{R}_{3}
. According to Theorem 3 in
[
KCL15
]
, when
\widehat{R}_{3}
is close enough to
R_{3}
, the output of the algorithm,
\widehat{u}_{i}
will close enough to
s_{i}V^{\top}\overline{w}_{i}^{*}
, where
s_{i}
is an unknown sign. Lemma
E.10
provides the error bound for
\|\widehat{R}_{3}-R_{3}\|
.

c) Recover the magnitude of
w_{i}^{*}
and the signs
s_{i},v_{i}^{*}
(Algorithm
4
).
For Algorithm
4
, we only consider homogeneous functions. Hence we can assume
v_{i}^{*}\in\{-1,1\}
and there exist some universal constants
c_{j}
such that
m_{j,i}=c_{j}\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p+1}
for
j=1,2,3,4
, where
p+1
is the degree of homogeneity. Note that different activations have different situations even under Assumption
5.3
. In particular, if
M_{4}=M_{2}=0
,
\phi(\cdot)
is an odd function and we only need to know
s_{i}v_{i}^{*}
. If
M_{3}=M_{1}=0
,
\phi(\cdot)
is an even function, so we don’t care about what
s_{i}
is.

Let’s describe the details for Algorithm
4
. First define two quantities
Q_{1}
and
Q_{2}
,

\displaystyle Q_{1}=M_{l_{1}}(I,\underbrace{\alpha,\cdots,\alpha}_{(l_{1}-1)\penalty\ \alpha\text{'s}})=\sum_{i=1}^{k}v_{i}^{*}c_{l_{1}}\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p+1}(\alpha^{\top}\overline{w}_{i}^{*})^{{l_{1}-1}}\overline{w}_{i}^{*},

(25)

\displaystyle Q_{2}=M_{l_{2}}(V,V,\underbrace{\alpha,\cdots,\alpha}_{(l_{2}-2)\penalty\ \alpha\text{'s}})=\sum_{i=1}^{k}v_{i}^{*}c_{l_{2}}\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p+1}(\alpha^{\top}\overline{w}_{i}^{*})^{{l_{2}-2}}(V^{\top}\overline{w}_{i}^{*})(V^{\top}\overline{w}_{i}^{*})^{\top},

(26)

where
l_{1}\geq 1
such that
M_{l_{1}}\neq 0
and
l_{2}\geq 2
such that
M_{l_{2}}\neq 0
. There are possibly multiple choices for
l_{1}
and
l_{2}
. We will discuss later on how to choose them.
Now we solve two linear systems.

\displaystyle z^{*}=\argmin_{z\in\mathbb{R}^{k}}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}z_{i}s_{i}\overline{w}_{i}^{*}-Q_{1}\right\|,\text{\penalty\ and\penalty\ }r^{*}=\argmin_{r\in\mathbb{R}^{k}}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}r_{i}V^{\top}\overline{w}_{i}^{*}(V^{\top}\overline{w}_{i}^{*})^{\top}-Q_{2}\right\|_{F}.

(27)

The solutions of the above linear systems are

\displaystyle z^{*}_{i}

\displaystyle=v_{i}^{*}s_{i}^{l_{1}}c_{l_{1}}\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p+1}(\alpha^{\top}s_{i}\overline{w}_{i}^{*})^{{l_{1}-1}},\text{\penalty\ and\penalty\ }r^{*}_{i}=v_{i}^{*}s_{i}^{l_{2}}c_{l_{2}}\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p+1}(\alpha^{\top}s_{i}\overline{w}_{i}^{*})^{{l_{2}-2}}.

We can approximate
s_{i}\overline{w}_{i}^{*}
by
V\widehat{u}_{i}
and approximate
Q_{1}
and
Q_{2}
by their empirical versions
\widehat{Q}_{1}
and
\widehat{Q}_{2}
respectively. Hence, in practice, we solve

\widehat{z}=\argmin_{z\in\mathbb{R}^{k}}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}z_{i}V\widehat{u}_{i}-\widehat{Q}_{1}\right\|,\text{\penalty\ and\penalty\ }\widehat{r}=\argmin_{r\in\mathbb{R}^{k}}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}r_{i}\widehat{u}_{i}\widehat{u}_{i}^{\top}-\widehat{Q}_{2}\right\|_{F}

(28)

So we have the following approximations,

\displaystyle\widehat{z}_{i}\approx v_{i}^{*}s_{i}^{l_{1}}c_{l_{1}}\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p+1}(\alpha^{\top}V\widehat{u}_{i})^{{l_{1}-1}},\text{\penalty\ and\penalty\ }\widehat{r}_{i}\approx v_{i}^{*}s_{i}^{l_{2}}c_{l_{2}}\|w_{i}^{*}\|^{p+1}(\alpha^{\top}V\widehat{u}_{i})^{{l_{2}-2}},\forall i\in[k].

In Lemma
E.13
and Lemma
E.14
, we provide robustness of the above two linear systems, i.e., the solution errors,
\|\widehat{z}-z^{*}\|
and
\|\widehat{r}-r^{*}\|
, are bounded under small perturbations of
\overline{w}_{i}^{*},Q_{1}
and
Q_{2}
.
Recall that the final goal is to approximate
\|w_{i}^{*}\|
and the signs
v_{i}^{*},s_{i}
.
Now we can approximate
\|w_{i}^{*}\|
by
(|\widehat{z}_{i}/(c_{l_{1}}(\alpha^{\top}V\widehat{u}_{i})^{{l_{1}-1}})|)^{1/(p+1)}
.
To recover
v_{i}^{*},s_{i}
, we need to note that if
l_{1}
and
l_{2}
are both odd or both even, we can’t recover both
v_{i}^{*}
and
s_{i}
. So we consider the following situations,

1.

If
M_{1}=M_{3}=0
, we choose
l_{1}=l_{2}=\min\{j\in\{2,4\}|M_{j}\neq 0\}
. Return
v_{i}^{(0)}=\mathrm{sign}(\widehat{r}_{i}c_{l_{2}})
and
s_{i}^{(0)}
being
-1
or
1
.

2.

If
M_{2}=M_{4}=0
, we choose
l_{1}=\min\{j\in\{1,3\}|M_{j}\neq 0\}
,
l_{2}=3
. Return
v_{i}^{(0)}
being
-1
or
1
and
s_{i}^{(0)}=\mathrm{sign}(v_{i}^{(0)}\widehat{z}_{i}c_{l_{1}})
.

3.

Otherwise, we choose
l_{1}=\min\{j\in\{1,3\}|M_{j}\neq 0\}
,
l_{2}=\min\{j\in\{2,4\}|M_{j}\neq 0\}
. Return
v_{i}^{(0)}=\mathrm{sign}(\widehat{r}_{i}c_{l_{2}})
and
s_{i}^{(0)}=\mathrm{sign}(v_{i}^{(0)}\widehat{z}_{i}c_{l_{1}})
.

The 1st situation corresponds to part 3 of Assumption
5.3
,where
s_{i}
doesn’t matter, and the 2nd situation corresponds to part 4 of Assumption
5.3
,
where only
s_{i}v_{i}^{*}
matters. So we recover
\|w_{i}^{*}\|
to some precision and
v_{i}^{*},s_{i}
exactly provided enough samples. The recovery of
w_{i}^{*}
and
v_{i}^{*}
then follows.

Algorithm 3

Power Method via Implicit Matrix-Vector Multiplication

1:

procedure

PowerMethod
(
\widehat{P}_{2},k
)

2:

C\leftarrow 3\|\widehat{P}_{2}\|
,
T\leftarrow
a large enough constant.

3:

Initial guess
\widehat{V}_{1}^{(0)}\in\R^{d\times k},\widehat{V}_{2}^{(0)}\in\R^{d\times k}

4:

for

t=1\to T

do

5:

\widehat{V}_{1}^{(t)}\leftarrow\textsc{QR}(C\widehat{V}_{1}^{(t-1)}+\widehat{P}_{2}\widehat{V}_{1}^{(t-1)})

\triangleright

\widehat{P}_{2}\widehat{V}_{1}^{(t-1)}
is not calculated directly, see Sec.
E.1
(a)

6:

\widehat{V}_{2}^{(t)}\leftarrow\textsc{QR}(C\widehat{V}_{2}^{(t-1)}-\widehat{P}_{2}\widehat{V}_{2}^{(t-1)})

7:

end

for

8:

for

j=1,2

do

9:

\widehat{V}_{j}^{(T)}\leftarrow\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{v}_{j,1}&\widehat{v}_{j,2}&\cdots&\widehat{v}_{j,k}\end{bmatrix}

10:

for

i=1\to k

do

11:

\lambda_{j,i}\leftarrow|\widehat{v}_{j,i}^{\top}\widehat{P}_{2}\widehat{v}_{j,i}|

\triangleright
Calculate the absolute of eigenvalues

12:

end

for

13:

end

for

14:

\pi_{1},\pi_{2},k_{1},k_{2}\leftarrow\textsc{TopK}(\lambda,k)

\triangleright

\pi_{j}:[k_{j}]\rightarrow[k]
and
k_{1}+k_{2}=k
, see Sec.
E.1
(a)

15:

for

j=1,2

do

16:

V_{j}\leftarrow\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{v}_{j,\pi_{j}(1)}&\widehat{v}_{1,\pi_{j}(2)}&\cdots&\widehat{v}_{j,\pi_{j}(k_{j})}\end{bmatrix}

17:

end

for

18:

\widetilde{V}_{2}\leftarrow\textsc{QR}((I-V_{1}V_{1}^{\top})V_{2})

19:

V\leftarrow[V_{1},\widetilde{V}_{2}]

20:

return

V

21:

end

procedure

Algorithm 4

Recovery of the Ground Truth Parameters of the Neural Network, i.e.,
w_{i}^{*}
and
v_{i}^{*}

1:

procedure

RecMagSign
(
V,\{\widehat{u}_{i}\}_{i\in[k]},S
)

2:

if

M_{1}=M_{3}=0

then

3:

l_{1}\leftarrow l_{2}\leftarrow\min\{j\in\{2,4\}|M_{j}\neq 0\}

4:

else

if

M_{2}=M_{4}=0

then

5:

l_{1}\leftarrow\min\{j\in\{1,3\}|M_{j}\neq 0\}
,
l_{2}\leftarrow 3

6:

else

7:

l_{1}\leftarrow\min\{j\in\{1,3\}|M_{j}\neq 0\}
,
l_{2}\leftarrow\min\{j\in\{2,4\}|M_{j}\neq 0\}
.

8:

end

if

9:

S_{1},S_{2}\leftarrow\textsc{Partition}(S,2)

\triangleright

|S_{1}|,|S_{2}|=\widetilde{\Omega}(d)

10:

Choose
\alpha
to be a random unit vector

11:

\widehat{Q}_{1}\leftarrow\E_{S_{1}}[Q_{1}]

\triangleright

\widehat{Q}_{1}
is the empirical version of
Q_{1}
(defined in Eq.(
25
))

12:

\widehat{Q}_{2}\leftarrow\E_{S_{2}}[Q_{2}]

\triangleright

\widehat{Q}_{2}
is the empirical version of
Q_{2}
(defined in Eq.(
26
))

13:

\widehat{z}\leftarrow\argmin_{z}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}z_{i}V\widehat{u}_{i}-\widehat{Q}_{1}\right\|

14:

\widehat{r}\leftarrow\argmin_{r}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}r_{i}\widehat{u}_{i}\widehat{u}_{i}^{\top}-\widehat{Q}_{2}\right\|_{F}

15:

v_{i}^{(0)}\leftarrow\mathrm{sign}(\widehat{r}_{i}c_{l_{2}})

16:
